def ls_projected(a, grad, S, base_step, rng=None, n_candidates=5):
   if grad_norm==0 return a,M
   a0=a
   c0=M
   best=a0; bestM=c0
   steps = base_step * [5,2,1,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05]
   for f in steps:
       t=f*base_step? (base can be change_target/norm)
       cand=proj_simplex(a0 - t*grad,S)
       c=conv(cand); m=c.max()
       if m < bestM: best...
       if m < c0*(1-1e-8)/? break? Could continue see lower.
   return best
```

Now our earlier optimization approach is relevant. But we discovered continuous connection. We can use shape parameterization (continuous f) to search global. Since n large, any smooth f can be sampled. Objective asymptotic O(f)=2 max_t (f*f)_t / (∫f)^2. Discrete finite adds O(1/n) effects. We can optimize f in low dimension using calculus and then discretize length 8192.

This might be the key: previous local search optimized 8192 variables but maybe stuck in local basin; a low-dimensional parametric family may find better global shape. Let's investigate possible f and objective.

We need find f≥0 on [0,1] minimizing 2 ||f*f||∞/(∫f)^2. This is convex? hmax convex, I^2 convex? Ratio not. But we can optimize.

Let's explore candidate functions analytically.

Let's design high-dimensional optimizers in detail, robust and top-level.

Helper functions:
- `fft_power_of_two(size)`? Use `1 << (size-1).bit_length()`.
- `convolution_self(a)`: full via FFT. To reduce negative tiny, return real.
- `evaluate_internal(a)`: n=len; S=a.sum(); if S<...; c=conv; return 2*n*c.max()/S^2. If we normalize sum to S=√(2n), return c.max(). Use same as evaluate_sequence no clipping? We'll ensure a≤1000, nonneg.
- `normalize_sum(a, S)`: np.clip(a,0,1000), s=sum, scale S/s; return.
- `project_simplex(v,S)`: as previous.
- `lse_grad_and_value(a,c,beta)`: cm=c.max(); e=exp(beta*(c-cm)); tot=e.sum(); if tot inf? Use stable. grad=2*ifft(fft(w)*conj(fft(a))). Need sizes fsize nextpow2(len(c)+n-1) where len(c)=2n-1. Wait previous used fsize len(w)+n-1. For gradient g_i=∑ w_j a_{j?} as derived. Use N=1<<(len(w)+len(a)-2).bit_length() (sum min1). Then grad full length N, take first n.
- `active_grad(a, weights)`: weights over c length; similar.
- `try_update_best(a, best_score, best_a)` top-level maybe with globals? To avoid closures, use list? But top-level can return tuple. In main, update.

Optimizers:
1. `run_adam_lse(a, n, max_time, rng, beta_start=50, beta_end=20000, beta_steps=200, lr=0.05, noise_start=0.005)`:
   - a normalized to S=√(2n). m,v zeros, t. β schedule over iterations (log). At each iter: c=conv(a), M. value= M (since normalized). If M<best update. λ=exp(β(c-M))/sum; grad=2... Normalize grad by mean? For Adam on simplex: update vraw = a - lr*(grad - mean(grad))? Since projection handles sum; subtract mean to keep. Clip tiny. Adam moments over vraw. a=proj(a+m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps), S). But Adam with projection can be unstable. Could use standard: a = project(a - lr*grad_centered).
   - Use adaptive lr: initial lr = 1e-3 * S/n? Let's think: a_i ~S/n=0.0156. We want changes ~1e-4. grad magnitude? LSE grad magnitude ~2*a ~0.03. step lr 0.1 gives 0.003 > typical? Need tune. Use gradient norm normalization: direction = (grad-mean)/norm2(grad-mean), step_size = eta * S/n. eta maybe 0.05-20. This makes move independent of β. Use Adam or plain.
   - For LSE high β, grad direction top. Use line search? Could evaluate one candidate with step eta, and if no improve reduce eta. This is robust.
   - Pseudocode:
```
a=normalize
eta=1.0
best=M
for iter in max_time:
  β = schedule
  c=conv(a); M=c.max()
  update best
  grad = lse_grad(beta)
  g = grad - grad.mean()
  gnorm = np.linalg.norm(g,1)/n? If small break.
  direction = g/gnorm maybe L1 normalized
  # try candidate steps eta* (S/n) /? Let move = eta * (S/n)
  cand=proj(a - move*direction, S)
  cm=conv(cand).max()
  if cm < M - 1e-12:
      a=cand; eta*=1.1 (clamp 100)
  else:
      # try smaller
      cm2=conv(proj(a - move*0.3*direction)).max()
      if cm2<M: a=...; eta*=0.7; else eta*=0.5; add noise if stagnant
  momentum? maybe skip
```
This line-search each iter two FFTs. Could be okay. Add noise when stagnant.
   - To use high β, direction exact top; η controls.
   - This is different from previous FISTA; might be better.

2. `run_fista_lse_improved`: Similar but momentum and step 1/β? Could keep as backup. Maybe implement simple FISTA with adaptive scaling, but line-search method may suffice.

3. `run_multi_top(a,n,max_time,rng)`:
   - Each iter use active λ candidates (top1, top5, top50, LSE β high) to find direction. For each direction, try step. Evaluate candidates; choose best new a. This is expensive but can refine. Use final phase maybe 200-500 iters. We can optimize by not trying all every iter, random cycle.
   - For top K, we can find top indices with `np.argpartition(c, K)[K-1:]`. To get threshold. For top1 just argmax. For K maybe 5,20,100,500. Weights uniform. For LSE β=5000. Compute grad for each. We can precompute fft(a) once per iter. For each weights, fft(weights), grad=2*ifft. Then line search with 2 candidates.
   - This can escape LSE basin.

4. `run_noise_perturb`: Add random smooth noise (Fourier modes) to current a, normalize, optimize. Use several seeds.

5. `run_spectral_param`: low-dim continuous optimizer.

Implementation details for `run_lse_search(a_iter, n, S, max_time, rng, beta_start=20, beta_end=50000, eta_start=0.5, use_active=False)`:
```
a=normalize_sum
best=M(a)
eta=eta_start
it=0; last_improve=0; stagnant=0; momentum=0 vector; t=1
while time< max_time:
  beta = schedule maybe cycle
  c=conv(a); M=c.max()
  update best
  # choose weights: LSE (or if stagnant top active)
  if use_top_refresh and it%100==0: weights = top softmax? else LSE.
  grad = weighted_gradient(a, weights)
  g = grad - np.mean(grad)
  gscale = np.max(np.abs(g)); if <1e-15 break
  d = g / gscale
  # try several moves? one move maybe
  move = eta * S / n
  cand = project_simplex(a - move*d, S)
  c2=conv(cand); M2=c2.max()
  if M2 < M:
     a=cand; eta=min(10,eta*1.2); last_improve=it
  else:
     # try smaller or alternate top
     cand2=project_simplex(a - move*0.3*d,S)
     M2=conv(cand2).max()
     if M2<M: a=cand2; eta*=0.7
     else:
        # maybe try top1 gradient direction
        ...
        eta*=0.7
  if it-last_improve>stagnation_limit:
        # reheat: beta lower, noise
        a=normalize(a+rng.normal(0, noise_scale,n))
        eta=0.3
        last_improve=it
```

Let's design code skeleton:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft

def next_pow2(m): return 1 << max(1, (m-1).bit_length())

def ft_convolve_self(a):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1; fsize=next_pow2(size)
    A=fft(a, fsize); c=np.real(ifft(A*A)); return c[:size]

def ft_weight_gradient(a, w):
    n=len(a); m=len(w); fsize=next_pow2(n+m-1)
    A=fft(a,fsize); W=fft(w,fsize)
    g=np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return 2.0*g

def normalize_sum(a,S):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: a=np.ones_like(a); s=a.sum()
    return a*(S/s)

def project_simplex(v,S):
   n=len(v); v=np.asarray(v,float).copy()
   if np.any(v < -1e12): v=np.maximum(v,0)
   w=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cs=np.cumsum(w); idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
   tc=(cs-S)/idx; valid=tc<=w
   if not valid.any(): rho=n
   else: rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])+1)
   theta=(cs[rho-1]-S)/rho
   out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
   # correct small sum errors
   diff=S-out.sum()
   if abs(diff)>0:
      # add to positive/out max? simple scale? Might break. We can adjust by adding diff/n if diff>0 else to positives.
      if diff>0:
         out += diff/n
      else:
         pos=out>0
         if pos.any(): out[pos] += diff/np.count_nonzero(pos)
         else: out += diff/n
   return out
```
Projection correction could introduce negative if subtract too much. Better compute theta with float; sum should close. Use no correction or minimal.

`objective(a)`: S=a.sum(); return 2*len(a)*c.max()/S^2. But if normalized sum √(2n), c.max.

`lse_weights(c,beta)`: cm=c.max(); z=beta*(c-cm); e=np.exp(z); tot=e.sum(); if not finite or tot<=0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1; else w=e/tot.

`top_weights(c,K,beta=NP.inf)`: ... For K maybe sort. Use argpartition if K < len and K>0. If K large, np.argsort? For K up to 1000, argpartition okay. For uniform active threshold, we can create weights where c >= M - tol. But top K enough.

`make_direction(a, c, rng, mode)`:
Modes: 'lse': weights lse, grad. 'topK': K weights. Return direction normalized max abs and grad_norm maybe.

`run_gradient_search(a, n, S, max_time, rng, beta_start, beta_end, beta_steps, eta0=0.5, top_cycle=None, reheat_every=200)`:
- We'll unify LSE and top. If top_cycle None use LSE only. If provided, cycle modes: each iter pick mode = list[it % len]. For each mode:
   - For LSE compute beta schedule or fixed.
   - For top K: beta_top maybe 0 uniform top K.
   - weights = ...
   - grad = ft_weight_gradient(a, weights)
   - center = grad - grad.mean()
   - d = center / max(abs(center))
   - move = eta * S / n
   - evaluate candidate1 = proj(a - move*d), M1.
   - if M1<M: a=cand1; eta=min(10,eta*1.05); improved=True
   - else try cand_small move*0.25, M2; if M2<M: a=cand2; eta*=0.7
   - else try maybe opposite? negative gradient for zero? no.
       add noise if stagnant.
   - If mode top and no improve, maybe reduce top K? Already.
- Stagnation: if it-last_improve>100: add noise scale = 0.02*S/n * exp(-stagnant/100)? Maybe 0.005; reset eta=0.3; lower beta? If beta schedule, can reheat by reset beta_start. We can manage beta as a variable: if stagnant, beta=max(10,beta/10).
- At intervals, evaluate exact a and if <best update. We compute c already.
- Constraint: a nonnegative and sum S by projection. Noise via project.

This line search does one or two conv per iter. For n=8192, 10000 iters ~2-4 conv =20k conv. Each conv 16384 FFT maybe 0.001-0.003s -> 20-60s. Good. 2400s can do many.

But if line search fails often, eta reduces and stagnates. Noise can escape.

Need choose eta. Let's set `base_move = 0.02 * S / n`? If eta=1, move 2% mean. Too big? Direction max abs normalized; some coordinates change by 2% mean=3e-4. That's okay. Let's detail `run_gradient_search` with modes. Top-level signature:
```
def run_gradient_search(a, S, max_time, rng, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=20000.0, eta0=1.0,
                        modes=None, top_sizes=None, stagnation_limit=80):
    if a is None: ...
    n=len(a); a=normalize_sum(a,S)
    c=ft_convolve_self(a); M=c.max(); best=M; best_a=a.copy()
    if modes is None: modes=['lse','top1','top3','top10','top50','top100']
    # build list mode specs: ('lse',None), ('topK',K), ('topKsoft',K,beta)
    tstart=time.monotonic(); it=0; eta=eta0; beta=beta_start; last_imp=0; step_count=0
    while time.monotonic()-tstart < max_time:
       it +=1
       mode=modes[it % len(modes)]
       c, M = ... but we can reuse c from previous candidate? After update, c is best candidate's c? We can store `c` and `M` for current a. Initially computed. After line search choose candidate, we have c_new, M_new; set c=M for next. If no improve but noise, compute new c.
       if M < best: update.
       # choose weights
       if mode starts with 'lse': beta = schedule or adapt; w=lse_weights(c,beta)
       elif 'top': K=int(part); w=top_weights(c,K, beta_soft if specified)
       grad=ft_weight_gradient(a,w)
       g=grad-np.mean(grad)
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(g)); if gmax<1e-14: break
       d=g/gmax
       move=eta*(S/n)
       cand=project_simplex(a - move*d, S)
       c2=ft_convolve_self(cand); M2=c2.max()
       improved=False
       if M2 < M - 1e-12:
           a=cand; c=c2; M=M2; eta=min(10.0, eta*1.1); improved=True
       else:
           small=0.25
           cand2=project_simplex(a - move*small*d, S)
           c2=ft_convolve_self(cand2); M2=c2.max()
           if M2 < M - 1e-12:
               a=cand2; c=c2; M=M2; eta=max(0.02, eta*0.6); improved=True
           else:
               # try top1 direction even if mode lse? maybe waste
               # reduce eta, add noise if stagnant
               eta=max(0.02, eta*0.6)
       if improved: last_imp=it
       stag=it-last_imp
       if stag>=stagnation_limit:
           # reheat
           noise_scale=0.05*(S/n)*np.exp(-stag/200.0)
           if noise_scale>1e-9:
              a_noise=project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n), S)
              c=ft_convolve_self(a_noise); M=c.max()
              a=a_noise
           beta=max(5.0, beta*0.3)
           eta=0.5
           last_imp=it
       # Increase beta if LSE improvements? If it%100==0 and beta<beta_end: beta*=1.2
       if it % 100 == 0 and beta < beta_end: beta = min(beta_end, beta*1.25)
    # final eval
    c=ft_convolve_self(a); M=c.max(); if M<best: ...
    return best_a, best
```

for k in range(1,K+1):

```
def make_a_from_theta(theta, basis, S):
   u = np.clip(theta, -8, 8)
   z = basis.dot(u)
   # Avoid overflow: subtract max
   z = z - np.max(z)
   phi = np.exp(z)
   s=phi.sum()
   if s<=0: phi=np.ones_like(phi)
   phi/=s
   return S*phi
```
This normalizes relative shape. Good.

`top_weights` implementation:
```
def top_weights(c,K,beta=0.0):
   n=len(c); w=np.zeros(n)
   if K<=1: w[np.argmax(c)]=1; return w
   if K>=n: w=np.ones(n); 
   else:
      idx=np.argpartition(c, n-K)[-K:]
      vals=c[idx]
      if beta<=0: w[idx]=1.0/n? sum1: w[idx]=1.0/K
      else: exp= np.exp(beta*(vals-vals.max())); w[idx]=exp/exp.sum()
   return w
```
For K large, argpartition works. For K=0? no.

`lse_weights`:
```
def lse_weights(c,beta):
   m=c.max(); z=beta*(c-m); z=np.clip(z,-50,0)
   e=np.exp(z); tot=e.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot<=0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
   else: w=e/tot
   return w
```

Projection correction: Let's implement robust:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    n=len(v)
    if n == 0: return v
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    theta_c = (cum - S)/idx
    valid = theta_c <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.nonzero(valid)[0]) + 1)
    theta = (cum[rho-1] - S)/rho
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    # Fix sum if drift
    diff = S - out.sum()
    if diff != 0.0:
       if n > 0:
           if diff > 0:
               out += diff / n
           else:
               pos = out > 0
               cnt = np.count_nonzero(pos)
               if cnt > 0:
                   out[pos] += diff / cnt
               else:
                   out += diff / n
    # final clip tiny negative
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    # if sum not exactly, scale positives
    s=out.sum()
    if s > 0 and abs(s-S)>1e-9*S:
       out *= S/s
    return out
```
Adding diff/n when diff>0 could make previously zero positive; okay. Subtracting from positives could make some negative if diff more than sum? diff small. Final scale. This projection may not be exact but good. In optimization, sum drift minimal.

Alternative use `out /= out.sum() * S` at end if no negatives. But if some zeros, scale okay. Use final scale always? It may alter max 0. Good. Do:
```
s=out.sum()
if s>0: out *= S/s
```
This ensures sum. If some zero, remain. If all zero (S>0 impossible? if v all negative, theta? It should produce uniform? Actually projection of negative to sum S gives some positives. If all zero, s=0, set out=S/n). Add guard.

`normalize_sum`:
```
def normalize_sum(a,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a=np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 0: a=np.ones(len(a))*S/len(a)
   else: a *= S/s
   np.clip(a,0,1000,out=a) # if S/len >1000? For n small maybe; but n large. If clip changes sum, maybe re-scale? For n=1 S=1.414 <1000.
   return a
```
If S/len >1000 (n<2), not. For n=2 S=2, len2 max2<1000. Fine.

def adaptive_lse_beta(c,beta_start,beta_end):
   m=c.max(); mlen=c.size; k=min(50,mlen); if k<=1: return beta_end
   vals=np.partition(c, mlen-k)[-k:]
   gap=m-vals.min()
   if gap <= 1e-12: gap=1e-12
   b=20.0/gap
   if b<beta_start: b=beta_start
   if b>beta_end: b=beta_end
   return b
```

`make_theta_inits(m, rng)`:
```
def make_theta_inits(m, rng):
    inits=[]
    inits.append(np.zeros(m))
    for scale in [0.3,0.6,1.0,1.6]:
        inits.append(rng.normal(0.0, scale, m))
    for i in range(min(6,m)):
        th=np.zeros(m)
        th[i]=float(rng.choice([-1.0,1.0]))*float(rng.uniform(0.5,1.5))
        inits.append(th)
    # combine
    for _ in range(3):
        th=rng.normal(0,0.8,m)
        if m>0: th[0]=float(rng.normal(0,1.0))
        inits.append(th)
    return inits
```
No lambda.

`run_param_search`:
```
def run_param_search(n, max_time, rng, K=10, n_inits=5):
    if max_time <=5 or n<16: return None, np.inf
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    basis = build_basis(n,K)
    m=basis.shape[1]
    inits=make_theta_inits(m,rng)
    while len(inits)>1 and max_time/len(inits)<20:
       inits=inits[:-1]
    per=max_time/len(inits)
    best_a=None; best_m=np.inf; t0=time.monotonic()
    for th in inits:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 >= max_time: break
       a,m = run_param_adam(th, basis, n, per, rng, 20.0, 50000.0, max_iter=40000)
       if a is not None and m<best_m: best_a=a; best_m=m
    return best_a,best_m
```
`build_basis` top-level.

def run_param_adam(theta, basis, n, max_time, rng, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=50000.0, max_iter=50000):
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    theta=np.asarray(theta,float).copy()
    a=make_a_from_theta(theta,basis,S)
    c=ft_convolve_self(a); M=c.max(); best=M; best_a=a.copy(); best_theta=theta.copy()
    m_ad=np.zeros_like(theta); v_ad=np.zeros_like(theta); lr=0.25; it=0; last=0; start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time and it<max_iter:
        it+=1
        if M<best: ... (c already)
        beta=adaptive_lse_beta(c,beta_start,beta_end)
        w=lse_weights(c,beta)
        ga=ft_weight_gradient(a,w)
        ga=ga-np.mean(ga)
        gt=basis.T.dot(a*ga)
        # if gt has nan break
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gt)): break
        m_ad=0.9*m_ad+0.1*gt; v_ad=0.999*v_ad+0.001*gt*gt
        bias1=1-0.9**it; bias2=1-0.999**it
        step = lr*m_ad/(np.sqrt(v_ad/(bias2))+1e-8) / bias1? Actually mhat=m/(1-b1); update step = lr*mhat/sqrt(vhat). Compute:
        m_hat=m_ad/bias1; v_hat=v_ad/bias2; step=lr*m_hat/(np.sqrt(v_hat)+1e-8)
        theta_try=theta-step
np.clip theta_try -8,8
        a_try=make_a_from_theta(theta_try,basis,S); c_try=ft_convolve_self(a_try); M_try=c_try.max()
        if M_try < M - 1e-13:
            theta=theta_try; a=a_try; c=c_try; M=M_try; lr=min(2.0, lr*1.1); last=it
        else:
            step2=0.5*step; theta_try=theta-step2; a_try=make_a...; c_try...
            if M_try2 < M: accept lr=max(0.01,lr*0.7); last=it
            else:
                lr=max(0.01, lr*0.6)
        stag=it-last
        if stag>100:
            # reheat
            lr=max(0.05, lr*0.5)
            theta = best_theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, theta.size)
            np.clip(theta,-8,8,out=theta)
            a=make_a_from_theta(theta,basis,S); c=ft_convolve_self(a); M=c.max()
            last=it
    c=ft_convolve_self(a); M=c.max(); best...
    return best_a,best_m
```

High-dim `run_gradient_search` similar. Need be careful with c current.

```
def run_gradient_search(a, S, max_time, rng, modes=None, eta0=1.0, top_beta=1000.0, max_iter=200000):
    n=len(a); a=normalize_sum(a,S)
    c=ft_convolve_self(a); M=c.max(); best=M; best_a=a.copy()
    if modes is None: modes=[...]
    eta=eta0; last=0; it=0; start=time.monotonic()
    while time... and it<max_iter:
       it+=1
       if M<best: update (but already)
       mode = modes[it % len(modes)]
       # weights
       if mode == 'lse':
          beta=adaptive_lse_beta(c,20,50000)
          w=lse_weights(c,beta)
          scale=1.0
       else:
          if mode.startswith('soft'):
             K=int(mode[4:]); beta_top=top_beta; scale=0.8
          else: # top
             K=int(mode[3:]); beta_top=0.0
             scale=0.5 if K<=5 else 0.8
          w=top_weights(c,K,beta_top)
       ga=ft_weight_gradient(a,w)
       g=ga-np.mean(ga); gmax=np.max(np.abs(g)); if gmax<1e-14: break
       d=g/gmax
       move=eta*scale*(S/n)
       cand=project_simplex(a-move*d,S)
       c2=ft_convolve_self(cand); M2=c2.max()
       improved=False
       if M2 < M - 1e-13:
          a=cand; c=c2; M=M2; eta=min(8.0,eta*1.1); improved=True
       else:
          small=0.25
          cand2=project_simplex(a-move*small*d,S)
          c2=ft_convolve_self(cand2); M2=c2.max()
          if M2 < M - 1e-13:
             a=cand2; c=c2; M=M2; eta=max(0.05,eta*0.7); improved=True
          else:
             eta=max(0.02,eta*0.6)
       if improved: last=it
       stag=it-last
       if stag>120:
          noise_scale=0.08*(S/n)*np.exp(-stag/150.0)
          if noise_scale > 1e-9:
             noise = rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n)
             a=project_simplex(a+noise,S)
             c=ft_convolve_self(a); M=c.max()
          eta=0.5
          last=it
    c=ft_convolve_self(a); M=c.max(); if M<best: ...
    return best_a,best
```
If no improve after several modes, eta decays to 0.02; move tiny; stag triggers noise. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Algorithm overview
------------------
1. Use the previous construction when available.
2. Run a low-dimensional spectral/log-shape parameter search directly on the
   sequence length.  This is used to find better global basins, especially
   asymmetric shapes that the previous dense local searches missed.
3. Polish with a monotone line-search projected-gradient method on the full
   sequence.  The gradient is a convex combination of gradients of active
   convolution peaks using:
       - adaptive log-sum-exp weights,
       - hard top-k active peaks,
       - soft top-k active peaks.
4. Perform several targeted restarts / perturbations and final peak-shaving.

The sequence is always normalized to sum sqrt(2n), so the objective is simply
max(conv(a,a)).  The sequence length is kept <= 16384 to avoid overly slow
evaluation and search.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def uniform_sequence(n):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)


def normalize_sum(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return uniform_sequence(a.size) * (S / np.sqrt(2.0 * a.size))
    a *= (S / s)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, S / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a *= (S / s)
    return a


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Project onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    S = float(S)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cs = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    tc = (cs - S) / idx
    valid = tc <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.nonzero(valid)[0])) + 1
    theta = (cs[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)

    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if abs(s - S) > 1e-12 * max(1.0, S):
        out *= (S / s)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def ft_convolve_self(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = next_pow2(size)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    return c[:size]


def objective_value(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    c = ft_convolve_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()) / (s * s))


def record_candidate(a, best_a, best_score):
    """Normalize, evaluate, and keep the best candidate."""
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_score
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if a.size < 16:
        return best_a, best_score
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(a.sum()) <= 1e-12:
        return best_a, best_score

    # Keep evaluation safe/fast.  Very long sequences are downsampled.
    if a.size > 16384:
        n2 = 16384
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
        x2 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n2)
        a = np.interp(x2, x1, a)

    n = a.size
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sum(a, S)
    c = ft_convolve_self(a)
    sc = float(c.max())
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return a.copy(), sc
    return best_a, best_score


def lse_weights(c, beta):
    """Softmax weights that approximate the largest coefficients of c."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = len(c)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return w
    cm = float(c.max())
    z = beta * (c - cm)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    tot = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot <= 0.0:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / tot
    return w


def adaptive_lse_beta(c, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=50000.0):
    """Choose LSE sharpness from the gap between top coefficients."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = len(c)
    if m == 0:
        return beta_start
    cm = float(c.max())
    k = min(64, m)
    if k <= 1:
        return beta_end
    vals = np.partition(c, m - k)[-k:]
    gap = cm - float(vals.min())
    if gap <= 1e-13:
        gap = 1e-13
    b = 20.0 / gap
    if b < beta_start:
        b = beta_start
    if b > beta_end:
        b = beta_end
    return float(b)


def top_weights(c, K, beta=0.0):
    """Uniform or soft weights on the top K convolution lags."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = len(c)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return w
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 1:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    if K >= m:
        w[:] = 1.0 / m
        return w

    idx = np.argpartition(c, m - K)[-K:]
    vals = c[idx]
    if beta <= 0.0:
        w[idx] = 1.0 / K
    else:
        vm = float(vals.max())
        z = np.clip(beta * (vals - vm), -50.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        tot = float(e.sum())
        if tot <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tot):
            w[idx] = 1.0 / K
        else:
            w[idx] = e / tot
    return w


def ft_weight_gradient(a, w):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k * c_k where c = conv(a,a).

    For c_k = sum_j a_j a_{k-j}, d c_k / d a_i = 2 a_{k-i} (when valid).
    Thus grad_i = 2 sum_j w_{i+j} a_j.
    The FFT below computes that with zero padding.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    fsize = next_pow2(2 * n)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    g = np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return 2.0 * g


def build_basis(n, K):
    """Fourier basis (cos and sin) with zero mean and unit standard deviation."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    cols = []
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        c = np.cos(k * np.pi * t)
        c -= float(c.mean())
        sc = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(c * c)))
        if sc > 1e-12:
            cols.append(c / sc)
        else:
            cols.append(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))

        s = np.sin(k * np.pi * t)
        s -= float(s.mean())
        ss = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s * s)))
        if ss > 1e-12:
            cols.append(s / ss)
        else:
            cols.append(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))
    if not cols:
        return np.zeros((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def make_a_from_theta(theta, basis, S):
    """Positive parameterized sequence a = S * exp(basis@theta) / sum(...)."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    u = np.clip(theta, -8.0, 8.0)
    z = basis.dot(u)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    phi = np.exp(z)
    ps = float(phi.sum())
    if ps <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ps):
        phi = np.ones_like(z)
        ps = float(phi.sum())
    phi /= ps
    return S * phi


def make_theta_inits(m, rng):
    """Several informative initial parameters for the log-Fourier model."""
    inits = []
    inits.append(np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64))

    # Learned-like starts: the first basis vector is cos(pi*x).
    for amp in [0.35, 0.55, 0.75, 0.95, 1.25, 1.60]:
        for sgn in [-1.0, 1.0]:
            th = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            if m > 0:
                th[0] = sgn * amp
            inits.append(th)

    # Add a second-even harmonic to help flatten sub-peaks.
    if m > 2:
        for a0 in [0.55, 0.75, 0.95, 1.15]:
            for a2 in [-0.70, -0.35, 0.35, 0.70]:
                th = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
                th[0] = a0
                th[2] = a2
                inits.append(th)
                th[0] = -a0
                inits.append(th)

    # Random starts of various amplitudes.
    for scale in [0.35, 0.7, 1.1, 1.7, 2.3]:
        inits.append(rng.normal(0.0, scale, m).astype(np.float64))

    # A few sparse single-mode starts.
    for i in range(min(8, m)):
        for _ in range(2):
            th = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            th[i] = float(rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0])) * float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.8))
            inits.append(th)

    return inits


def run_param_adam(theta, basis, n, max_time, rng, max_iter=80000):
    """
    Adam-like line-search optimization in a log-Fourier parameter space.
    The sequence is always a positive shape scaled to sum sqrt(2n).
    """
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)

    a = make_a_from_theta(theta, basis, S)
    c = ft_convolve_self(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = M
    best_theta = theta.copy()

    m_ad = np.zeros_like(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    v_ad = np.zeros_like(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    lr = 0.25
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    it = 0
    last_improve = 0
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time and it < max_iter:
        it += 1

        if M < best_m:
            best_m = M
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_theta = theta.copy()
            last_improve = it

        beta = adaptive_lse_beta(c, 20.0, 50000.0)
        w = lse_weights(c, beta)
        ga = ft_weight_gradient(a, w)
        ga = ga - float(np.mean(ga))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(ga)):
            break

        # d a_i / d theta_j = a_i * basis[i,j], because basis columns are zero-mean.
        gt = basis.T.dot(a * ga)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gt)):
            break

        m_ad = b1 * m_ad + (1.0 - b1) * gt
        v_ad = b2 * v_ad + (1.0 - b2) * gt * gt
        bias1 = 1.0 - b1 ** it
        bias2 = 1.0 - b2 ** it
        m_hat = m_ad / bias1
        v_hat = v_ad / bias2
        step = lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + 1e-8)

        accepted = False

        # Full step.
        th_try = np.clip(theta - step, -8.0, 8.0)
        a_try = make_a_from_theta(th_try, basis, S)
        c_try = ft_convolve_self(a_try)
        M_try = float(c_try.max())
        if M_try < M - 1e-13:
            theta = th_try
            a = a_try
            c = c_try
            M = M_try
            lr = min(2.0, lr * 1.12)
            last_improve = it
            accepted = True
        else:
            # Half step.
            th_try = np.clip(theta - 0.5 * step, -8.0, 8.0)
            a_try = make_a_from_theta(th_try, basis, S)
            c_try = ft_convolve_self(a_try)
            M_try = float(c_try.max())
            if M_try < M - 1e-13:
                theta = th_try
                a = a_try
                c = c_try
                M = M_try
                lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.7)
                last_improve = it
                accepted = True
            else:
                lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.6)

        if not accepted and it - last_improve > 90:
            # Reheat: return to the best theta and add moderate parameter noise.
            scale = 0.35 * max(1.0, np.sqrt(it - last_improve) / 20.0)
            theta = best_theta + rng.normal(0.0, scale, theta.size)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -8.0, 8.0)
            a = make_a_from_theta(theta, basis, S)
            c = ft_convolve_self(a)
            M = float(c.max())
            lr = max(0.05, lr * 0.55)
            last_improve = it

    c = ft_convolve_self(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M < best_m:
        best_m = M
        best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_m


def run_param_search(n, max_time, rng, K=10, n_inits=6):
    """Low-dimensional search over a log-Fourier positive shape."""
    if n <= 0 or max_time <= 5.0:
        return None, np.inf
    n = int(min(16384, max(256, n)))
    basis = build_basis(n, K)
    inits = make_theta_inits(basis.shape[1], rng)

    while len(inits) > 2 and max_time / len(inits) < 18.0:
        inits = inits[:-1]
    if len(inits) == 0:
        return None, np.inf

    per = max_time / float(len(inits))
    best_a = None
    best_m = np.inf
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    for th in inits:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 >= max_time:
            break
        a, val = run_param_adam(th, basis, n, per, rng, max_iter=60000)
        if a is not None and val < best_m:
            best_a = a
            best_m = val

    return best_a, best_m


def run_gradient_search(a, S, max_time, rng, modes=None, eta0=1.0, max_iter=300000):
    """
    Full-sequence monotone projected-gradient search.

    Each iteration chooses one active-peak weighting scheme, computes its
    gradient, normalizes it, and runs a two-try line search using exact
    convolution peak value.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return a, np.inf
    S = float(S)
    a = normalize_sum(a, S)

    c = ft_convolve_self(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = M

    if modes is None or len(modes) == 0:
        modes = ['lse', 'top1', 'top5', 'soft10', 'soft100', 'soft500']

    eta = float(eta0)
    it = 0
    last_improve = 0
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time and it < max_iter:
        it += 1

        if M < best_m:
            best_m = M
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_improve = it

        mode = modes[it % len(modes)]

        if mode == 'lse':
            beta = adaptive_lse_beta(c, 20.0, 50000.0)
            w = lse_weights(c, beta)
            scale = 1.0
        elif mode.startswith('soft'):
            K = int(mode[4:])
            w = top_weights(c, K, beta=2500.0)
            scale = 0.85
        elif mode.startswith('top'):
            K = int(mode[3:])
            w = top_weights(c, K, beta=0.0)
            scale = 0.55 if K <= 5 else 0.85
        else:
            w = lse_weights(c, adaptive_lse_beta(c, 20.0, 50000.0))
            scale = 1.0

        ga = ft_weight_gradient(a, w)
        g = ga - float(np.mean(ga))
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(gmax):
            break

        d = g / gmax
        move = eta * scale * (S / n)

        cand = project_simplex(a - move * d, S)
        c2 = ft_convolve_self(cand)
        M2 = float(c2.max())
        improved = False

        if M2 < M - 1e-13:
            a = cand
            c = c2
            M = M2
            eta = min(8.0, eta * 1.12)
            improved = True
        else:
            cand = project_simplex(a - 0.25 * move * d, S)
            c2 = ft_convolve_self(cand)
            M2 = float(c2.max())
            if M2 < M - 1e-13:
                a = cand
                c = c2
                M = M2
                eta = max(0.04, eta * 0.68)
                improved = True
            else:
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.58)

        if improved:
            last_improve = it

        stagnation = it - last_improve
        if stagnation > 140:
            # Escape local basin with a decaying random kick.
            nscale = 0.08 * (S / n) * float(np.exp(-stagnation / 180.0))
            if nscale > 1e-12:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, nscale, n)
                a = project_simplex(a + noise, S)
                c = ft_convolve_self(a)
                M = float(c.max())
            eta = 0.65
            last_improve = it

    c = ft_convolve_self(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M < best_m:
        best_m = M
        best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_m


def basic_transforms(a, rng):
    """A few cheap global/amplitude perturbations for extra diversity."""
    out = []
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return out
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)

    out.append(normalize_sum(a[::-1].copy(), S))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    for p in [0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0]:
        b = a * (1e-8 + (0.05 + 0.95 * t) ** p)
        out.append(normalize_sum(b, S))
        b = a * (1e-8 + (0.95 + 0.05 * (1.0 - t)) ** p)
        out.append(normalize_sum(b, S))

    # Low-frequency smooth random perturbations.
    for _ in range(3):
        phases = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, 4)
        amps = rng.uniform(-0.25, 0.25, 4)
        noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 5):
            noise += amps[k - 1] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + phases[k - 1])
        b = a * (1.0 + noise)
        out.append(normalize_sum(b, S))

    # Sparse/threshold variants.
    for q in [0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35]:
        if n > 100:
            th = float(np.quantile(a, q))
            b = a.copy()
            b[a < th] = 0.0
            if b.sum() > 0.0:
                out.append(normalize_sum(b, S))

    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    safety = 90.0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(120.0, budget_s - safety)

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # Load previous construction if available.
    given = None
    try:
        given = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if given.size > 0:
            given = np.clip(given, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if given.size > 16384:
                n2 = 16384
                x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, given.size)
                x2 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n2)
                given = np.interp(x2, x1, given)
            best_a, best_score = record_candidate(given, best_a, best_score)
            print("Loaded previous construction: n=%d, score=%.10f" % (len(best_a), best_score))
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = uniform_sequence(8192)
        best_score = objective_value(best_a)
        print("No previous construction found; starting uniform n=8192, score=%.10f" % best_score)

    n_best = len(best_a)
    print("Current best: n=%d, internal objective=%.10f" % (n_best, best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: Low-dimensional global/shell search at one or two lengths.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        total_param = min(520.0, max(120.0, rem * 0.27))

        n_try_list = []
        if n_best < 4096:
            n_try_list.append(8192)
            if n_best >= 1024:
                n_try_list.append(n_best)
        elif n_best <= 8192:
            n_try_list.append(n_best)
            if n_best * 2 <= 16384:
                n_try_list.append(int(n_best * 2))
        else:
            n_try_list.append(n_best)

        # Keep unique and valid.
        cleaned = []
        for x in n_try_list:
            x = int(min(16384, max(1024, x)))
            if x not in cleaned:
                cleaned.append(x)
        n_try_list = cleaned[:2]

        per = total_param / float(max(1, len(n_try_list)))
        for idx, ntry in enumerate(n_try_list):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 120.0:
                break
            K = 10 if idx == 0 else 8
            nin = 7 if idx == 0 else 4
            print("=== Param search: n=%d, time=%.0fs, K=%d, inits=%d ===" %
                  (ntry, per, K, nin))
            pa, pv = run_param_search(ntry, per, rng, K=K, n_inits=nin)
            if pa is not None:
                old = best_score
                best_a, best_score = record_candidate(pa, best_a, best_score)
                print("Param n=%d value=%.10f -> best=%.10f" % (ntry, pv, best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: Full-sequence gradient polish on the current best.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(850.0, max(120.0, rem * 0.36))
        n_best = len(best_a)
        S_best = np.sqrt(2.0 * n_best)
        modes_main = ['lse', 'top1', 'top5', 'soft10', 'soft100', 'soft500', 'top10', 'lse', 'top3']
        print("=== Main gradient search: n=%d, time=%.0fs ===" % (n_best, alloc))
        pa, pv = run_gradient_search(best_a, S_best, alloc, rng, modes=modes_main, eta0=1.0)
        old = best_score
        best_a, best_score = record_candidate(pa, best_a, best_score)
        print("Main gradient value=%.10f -> best=%.10f" % (pv, best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: Gradient search from an independent/perturbed candidate.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        # Candidate 2: previous construction if it is different length, else perturbation.
        if given is not None and len(given) != len(best_a):
            cand2 = given
            tag = "previous construction"
        else:
            n_best = len(best_a)
            S_best = np.sqrt(2.0 * n_best)
            t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_best)
            noise = np.zeros(n_best, dtype=np.float64)
            for k in range(1, 6):
                noise += rng.uniform(-0.25, 0.25) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
            cand2 = normalize_sum(best_a * (1.0 + noise), S_best)
            tag = "perturbed best"

        cand2, sc2 = record_candidate(cand2, None, np.inf)
        if cand2 is not None:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            alloc = min(600.0, max(90.0, rem * 0.26))
            n2 = len(cand2)
            S2 = np.sqrt(2.0 * n2)
            modes_2 = ['top1', 'soft10', 'soft100', 'lse', 'top5', 'soft500', 'top3', 'lse']
            print("=== Second gradient search (%s): n=%d, time=%.0fs ===" % (tag, n2, alloc))
            pa, pv = run_gradient_search(cand2, S2, alloc, rng, modes=modes_2, eta0=0.9)
            best_a, best_score = record_candidate(pa, best_a, best_score)
            print("Second gradient value=%.10f -> best=%.10f" % (pv, best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: Random restarts from best with short searches.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        n_best = len(best_a)
        S_best = np.sqrt(2.0 * n_best)
        mean_a = float(np.mean(best_a))
        for restart in range(4):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 80.0:
                break
            alloc = min(80.0, max(12.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.10))
            if alloc < 10.0:
                break

            nscale = (0.04 + 0.03 * restart) * mean_a
            pert = best_a + rng.normal(0.0, nscale, n_best)
            pert = normalize_sum(pert, S_best)
            modes_r = ['lse', 'top1', 'soft50', 'top5', 'soft500', 'lse']
            pa, pv = run_gradient_search(pert, S_best, alloc, rng, modes=modes_r, eta0=0.8)
            old = best_score
            best_a, best_score = record_candidate(pa, best_a, best_score)
            print("Restart %d: value=%.10f -> best=%.10f" % (restart + 1, pv, best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: Quick affordable transformations on the best sequence.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        transforms = basic_transforms(best_a, rng)
        for idx, tr in enumerate(transforms):
            old = best_score
            best_a, best_score = record_candidate(tr, best_a, best_score)
            if best_score < old:
                print("Transform %d improved to %.10f" % (idx, best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 6: Final surgical peak reduction.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        alloc = max(5.0, min(120.0, rem * 0.45))
        n_best = len(best_a)
        S_best = np.sqrt(2.0 * n_best)
        final_modes = ['top1', 'top3', 'soft10', 'soft50', 'lse', 'top1', 'soft20']
        pa, pv = run_gradient_search(best_a, S_best, alloc, rng, modes=final_modes, eta0=0.45)
        best_a, best_score = record_candidate(pa, best_a, best_score)
        print("Final polish value=%.10f -> best=%.10f" % (pv, best_score))

    # Final normalization and safety.
    if best_a is None or len(best_a) == 0:
        best_a = uniform_sequence(8192)
        best_score = objective_value(best_a)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 1e-12)
    n_final = len(best_a)
    S_final = np.sqrt(2.0 * n_final)
    best_a = normalize_sum(best_a, S_final)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    print("Returning length=%d, internal estimate=%.10f" % (len(final_list), best_score))

    # Final verification against the official evaluator if available.
    try:
        ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if np.isfinite(ver):
            print("evaluate_sequence(final)=%.10f" % ver)
            if ver < best_score:
                best_score = ver
        else:
            print("evaluate_sequence rejected final; using uniform fallback")
            fallback = uniform_sequence(8192)
            final_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]
            best_score = objective_value(fallback)
    except Exception:
        print("evaluate_sequence unavailable or failed during local check; using internal estimate")

    if not final_list:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in uniform_sequence(8192)]

    return final_list
```
